## Document 007: 10月非农数据点评：就业难唱独角戏

Source PDF: all report pdf/10月非农数据点评：就业难唱独角戏.pdf
Report source: 国信证券
Date: 2026-11-08
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

事项：
美东时间 11 月 6 日，美国劳工部统计局（BLS）发布 10 月非农就业报告。新增非农就业 16.5 万人，显
著高于市场预期的 6.5万人；失业率下行至 4.1%，低于市场预期的 4.2%。
评论：
图1：非农数据总览
资料来源：Wind、国信证券经济研究所整理
美国 10 月非农大幅超预期。美国 10 月新增非农就业 16.5 万人，高于预期的 6.5 万人，8-9 月合计上修
8.4万人，显示此前招聘放缓的程度被高估。商务服务、私人教育医疗和运输仓储贡献约九成净新增就业，
咨询、临时用工、医疗及快递招聘构成主要支撑，部分消费服务和地产相关行业仍然承压。我们认为，
美国就业“喜忧参半”，喜的是美国总体就业市场十分强劲，而忧的是就业质量仍存在瑕疵。失业率降至
4.1%，劳动参与率升至 61.9%，就业增长快于劳动力扩张，且裁员压力有限，说明用工需求仍有韧性。不
过，非自愿兼职增加、广义失业率 U6升至 7.9%，表明部分劳动者仍面临工时不足，数据成色有瑕疵。
数据公布后，12 月加息预期升温，但资产表现分化，市场未全面交易持续收紧。站在当前时点，市场对

就业数据的反应存在两难：过弱容易引发衰退交易，过强又会增加激进加息的担忧。本月数据明显偏强，
2026年11月08日

### Page 2

缓解了增长担忧，也使政策收紧重新成为市场关注点。不过，从盘中表现看，其影响主要集中于强化 12
月加息预期（12 月加息 25 个基点的概率升至 76.8%），尚未体现为对明年更大幅度收紧的全面定价。美
债收益率冲高后，长端回吐涨幅，短端仍高于公布前水平；美元走强、黄金下跌，美股开盘后反弹但随
后有所回落。就业韧性缓解了增长担忧，同时增加了利率对资产估值的约束。
这份非农是否属于沃什在 10 月会议上所说的“需要等待的更多信息”？我们认为，确实如此。就业回暖
印证了经济韧性，减轻了进一步收紧的就业顾虑，也增加了 12 月加息的理由。但它是否足以改变政策路
径，成为决定后续加息幅度的关键变量？我们认为，尚不足以。一是非农数据修订较大，单月改善仍需
多项指标相互印证，或连续数月形成趋势；二是在就业保持韧性、价格压力尚未消退的背景下，通胀仍
是政策权衡的重点，就业改善提供了加息空间，却不能单独决定加息幅度；三是对市场而言，民主党掌
控国会后的财政政策变化、AI 投资及回报前景，会更直接地影响中期增长和利率预期，其重要性超过单

月宏观数据。我们维持 12 月加息 25个基点、明年再加息一次的基准判断。
 新增非农：远超预期，招聘改善仍有结构分化
10 月新增非农就业 16.5 万人，显著高于 6.5 万人的市场预期，且 8-9 月合计上修 8.4 万人，显示招聘
修复强于此前判断。我们认为，企业经营活动扩张与用工需求回补是招聘改善的重要支撑，制造业和服
务业 ISM就业指数均处于扩张区间，也提供了旁证。此外，年末旺季招聘为就业提供有力支撑。
从结构看，医疗、商务服务和物流主导本月就业增长，但行业改善仍不均衡。10 月私营部门新增就业
15.9 万人，其中服务业增加 14.4 万人，商品生产增加 1.5万人；政府部门增加 0.6万人。专业商业服务
增加 7.0万人，主要由咨询和临时用工带动；私人教育医疗增加 5.3万人，其中医疗保健增加 4.16 万人，
医院及居家医疗是主要来源；运输仓储增加 2.48 万人，主要由快递招聘支撑，仓储就业则略有减少。上
述三个行业合计贡献约九成净新增就业，而休闲酒店、批发和建筑分别减少 1.3万、0.91万和 0.3万人，

表明招聘修复尚未覆盖全部消费服务及地产相关环节。
图2：新增非农就业趋势 图3：新增非农就业季节性因素

资料来源：Wind、国信证券经济研究所整理 资料来源：Wind、国信证券经济研究所整理
 失业率：就业吸纳改善，广义失业率逆势上升
10 月就业吸纳改善，失业率小幅下降。失业率由 4.2%降至 4.1%，劳动参与率同步升至 61.9%，说明更多
人进入求职市场后，就业增长仍能消化新增供给。从绝对就业人数上看，就业增长快于劳动力增长。10
月劳动力增加 42.1万人，就业增加 46.3万人，失业人数减少 4.1 万人，就业人口比升至 59.3%。我们认
为，失业率回落伴随着参与率和就业人口比同步上升，反映新增劳动力得到更充分吸收。
不过，广义失业率 U6 由 7.6%升至 7.9%，表明岗位数量改善的同时，部分劳动者仍面临工时不足，就业
修复的质量弱于总量表现。失业者分项中，职位流失者及临时工作结束者合计减少 3.4 万人，其中临时
裁员人数减少 7.9 万人、永久职位流失者减少 1.7 万人，临时工作结束者增加 6.2 万人。再进入者增加

0.8万人、新进入者减少 4.0万人，可见新增求职者积压有所缓解。
我们认为，从 10月非农数据来看，美国就业喜忧参半。喜的是新增就业回升、失业率下降，且裁员压力
有限，用工需求仍有韧性；忧的是非自愿兼职增加、广义失业率上升，就业质量改善滞后于数量增长。
往后看，我们更关注国会易手后的预算拨款和政府采购。若两党分歧拖慢项目审批与资金到位，依赖公
共项目的工程建设、科研及专业服务企业将承受订单兑现压力，招聘与就业也将承压。
图6：美国失业率 图7：美国劳动参与率与新增登记失业人数
资料来源：Wind、国信证券经济研究所整理 资料来源：Wind、国信证券经济研究所整理

 往后看：近期加息预期强化，中期路径仍取决于通胀
非农数据公布后，市场上调 12 月加息预期。12 月加息 25 个基点的概率升至 76.8%，较前一日提高约 10
个百分点，表明就业回暖增强了市场对联储继续收紧的判断。2027 年仍定价加息一次，市场对本轮加息
的终点仍未形成一致预期。资产价格先交易加息预期升温，随后出现分化，强非农尚未推动远期利率全
面上行。数据发布后 1 小时，2 年期美债收益率上行约 2.9个基点，10 年和 30 年期收益率则在冲高后分
别回落约 0.4 和 2.3 个基点；美元小幅走强，黄金下跌约 34 美元。纳指和标普 500 开盘后一度较开盘上
涨约 0.7%和 0.8%，随后回吐部分涨幅。
我们认为，此次非农对市场的直接影响有限，但确实是沃什等待的重要数据之一。就业回暖进一步印证
经济韧性，使联储能够更多关注通胀，也增加了 12 月加息的理由。不过，油价回落并不意味着通胀压力

已经消退，核心商品和服务价格仍有黏性。对市场而言，国会易手后的财政政策走向、AI 投资能否持续，
是比单月非农更重要的中期主线。这些变化会先影响市场对未来增长和利率的判断，再逐步体现在就业
等宏观数据中。
我们延续先前判断，预计 12 月加息 25 个基点，明年加息一次。我们判断，油价回落将逐步缓解整体通
胀压力，但核心商品成本传导尚未结束，服务价格也仍有黏性，即便能源降温难以成为美联储立即停止
加息的充分理由。当前就业韧性减轻了联储对进一步收紧冲击经济的顾虑，使其能够更多关注价格稳定。
我们暂以 12 月和明年各加息一次为基准，是否进一步收紧，仍取决于价格压力消退的程度。
图10：CME利率期货数据
资料来源：Wind、国信证券经济研究所整理

1 群益投顧 
國巨 (2327 TT) 整體 3Q26 財報表現優異，其中營收為 495.3 億元 (QoQ +11.4% 、 YoY 
+49.7%) ，主因受惠 AI 帶 動 相 關 被 動 元 件 增 溫 ， 其 中AI 占比較 2Q26 的 16% 提升至
近 20% ； EPS 5.83 元，優於 預期 。 然 展 望 4Q26 ，受 PC 市 場 疲 軟 及 消 費 性 電 子 需 求
放緩抵銷預期 AI 於 4Q 帶 來 的 強 勁 增 長，國 巨 4Q26 營收將從預估季增雙位數下修至
微 幅 季 增，不 過 因 VR 將於 2027 年 正 式 放 量，被 動 產 能 供 需 將 會 更 緊，故 維 持 整 體 被
動 元 件 漲 價 趨 勢 向 上 ， 看 法 為 短 空 長 多 。

國巨 3Q26 法 說 會 重 點：  
國巨 (2327 TT) 於昨 (29) 舉辦 3Q26 法 說 會 ， 整 體 3Q26 財 報 表 現 優 異 ， 其 中
營收為 495.3 億元 (QoQ +11.4% 、 YoY +49.7%) ， 主 因 受 惠 AI 帶 動 相 關 被 動
元 件 增 溫 ， 其 中 AI 占比較 2Q26 的 16% 提 升 至 近 20% ； 毛 利 率 因 產 品 組 合
轉佳至 41.0%(QoQ +2.5ppts 、 YoY +4.8ppts) ； OPM 29.5%(QoQ +3.1ppts 、
YoY +6.6ppts) ； 歸 屬 母 公 司 稅 後 淨 利 為 119.8 億元 (QoQ +27.2% 、 YoY 
+88.5%) ；EPS 5.83 元 ， 優 於 群 益 預 估 的 5.15 元 。 然 展 望 4Q26 ，受 PC 市場
疲 軟 及 消 費 性 電 子 需 求 放 緩 抵 銷 預 期 AI 於 4Q 帶 來 的 強 勁 增 長 ，BB ratio
略 較 前 季 呈 現 微 幅 下 滑，國 巨 4Q26 營 收 將 從 預 估 季 增 雙 位 數 下 修 至 微 幅 季
增 ， 不 過 因 VR 將於 2027 年 正 式 放 量 ， 被 動 產 能 供 需 將 會 更 緊 ， 故 維 持 整

2 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

Realtek (2379.TW)

6
Realtek
Company description
Founded in 1987, Realtek is a leading fabless semiconductor with
headquarters in Hsinchu, Taiwan. It has an extensive global footprint with
operational offices in China, Japan, Korea, Singapore, and the US. Realtek's
product portfolio is well-diversified; generally, it can be categorized into three
business groups: Communications Network (Wi-Fi, Bluetooth, automotive
Ethernet, PC Ethernet, switch, etc.), Computer Peripherals (audio codec,

clock IC, card reader, IP cam SoC, USB  hub controller, Type-C controller, SSD
controller) and Multimedia (TV SoC, LCD controller, STB SoC, etc.).
Investment strategy
We rate the shares Buy. We believe Realtek is well-positioned to ride on
continued specs upgrade demand for WiFi, switch and 10G PON as well as the
strong advancements in the automotive and Io T sectors. In the long term, we
believe Realtek's cost-effective, high-performance, and comprehensive wired

and wireless solutions should become more critical with the growing
connectivity requirement at the edge.
Valuation
Our target price for Realtek is set at NT$900 based on our target PE multiple
of 20x to our 2027 EPS. We believe our PER of 20x, higher than its 3-year
forward PER average, is justified by its solid growth opportunity in automotive
and switch/PON business. We also believe its continued efforts in products
mix optimization should help to support its margins expansion. At our target

price, the shares would trade at a 2026/27E P/B of 8x/6x.
Risks
Key downside risks to our target price include: 1) Slowdown in technology
migration for its networking business; 2) Weaker-than-expected growth
momentum for automotive Ethernet sales; 3) Worse-than-expected PC/NB
and consumer electronic demand; 4) Slower-than-expected GM expansion
due to pricing competition from peers; and 5) Higher-than-expected memory
cost burden to dampen the order outlook from its networking and PC/NB

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

GS Factor Profile  ______________________________

We hosted EMC’s 3Q26 post result call on October 29th. Key 
takeaways include: 
(1) EMC mentioned its proactive capacity expansion plans in coming 
years (capacity will go up to 15.25mn sheets/month by end of 2030 
vs. 5.85mn sheets/month now) are still not enough to support all the 
LTA they signed for the next 4 years, implying a solid demand 
outlook without much risk on oversupply.  
(2) In terms of the CCL pricing, the company mentioned it’s doing 
the second round of the high-end CCL (M7+) pricing hike this year, 
with 5-20% pricing hike ratio, which not only on M7/M8, but also on 
M8+/M9 grade CCL.  
(3) For 4Q26, EMC mentioned the conservative estimate on revenue 
growth rate should be at least 15-20% QoQ, with GM could at least 
expand to 38.0%-39.5% (note that the company estimated 3Q rev 
could grow 15% QoQ and GM could be 33.5%-36.5% back to 2Q26 
result call - here, but the actual outcome was revenue up 29% QoQ 
and GM hit 37.7%).  
(4) For substrate material, the company mentioned it is focusing on 
the high-end ABF substrate core-layer CCL (BT type, but for ABF 
substrate), and will hold 50% market share for the MTK TPU v8 ABF 
substrate. EMC also mentioned that it is now working with several 
customers on high end ABF used CCL qualification projects which 
the company expects to start shipment from 4Q27. 
(5) For the substrate material capacity, EMC now holds 300k 
sheets/month capacity, but will expand the capacity to at least 
1.2mn sheets/month by end of 2027. EMC mentioned the proactive 
expansion is their strategy on always prepare capacity ahead of 
customers’ demand, and mentioned these new capacity can also be 
used to produce high-end M9/M10/M11 CCL if the demand cannot 
fill all the capacity.

EMC call takeaways: Solid demand outlook will continue all the way to 2030, with 
more earnings’ upside from new substrate CCL business

### Page 3

(6) In terms of the profitability of the substrate material, the company mentioned the
GM of this product will be at least 50%+, despite its relatively competitive pricing
compares to the market leader who has 65%+ GM on substrate material.
(7) EMC mentioned it does see AI customers start qualifying second or third sources to
improve the stability of the supply chain, which should give more room for competitors
to enter the market. However, the company continues to target 50% AI CCL market

share in the long term, and believes its best in class product quality and deep
cooperation with PCB/CSP customers can see it continue to hold the leadership position
in this high-end industry.
Investment view - read across for high-end CCL players
We maintain our positive view on the high-end CCL players, including EMC and TUC,
while we see both companies are enjoying not only favorable pricing conditions on
high-end CCL (due to the wide supply demand gap) but also the accelerating product

mix improvement speed (we expect M9 massive adoption to start from 2H27 with 50%+
adoption rate for next generation AI server, and expect EMC to start M10 CCL product
shipment from 2H27 with mass volume shipment to start from 2028). See our Oct 28
report.
Both EMC and TUC mentioned the proactive capacity expansion they are planning in the
next 3-5 years (both companies will have 150-250% more capacity in 2029-2030 vs.
today) are still not enough to support the high-end AI/LEO/switching customers’

demand, suggesting an even more favorable pricing outlook. We expect the high-end
CCL pricing will go up by 40%+ YoY in 2027 considering the increasing tightness
condition of the high-end CCL industry (vs. 20-30% YoY 2026), which should drive both
company’s GM further in coming years.
In terms of competition, while we already model EMC’s AI CCL market share to decease
from 60-70% in 2026 to ~50% in the next three years, we do believe EMC’s earnings will

grow by 90% 2026-29 CAGR even with this 50% market share. For TUC, we expect it
should be the key share gainer from EMC considering CSPs duo source policy (we think
TUC would be a strong option due to not only its better technology skillset but also its
proactive expansion plans while other high-end CCL players are not expanding capacity
or only expanding slightly on the capacity), and we expect the company will have
multiple AI customers including AI ASIC/GPU end customers from 2H27.

Overall, we are even more positive on EMC/TUC compared to 3 months ago, after seeing
customers solid plans on high-end CCL LTA and customers’ design upgrade on CCL spec
(we had expected M10 MP would happen in 2029 or beyond only). We believe
EMC/TUC’s key competition advantage is not only from the leadership position in the
high-end CCL market, but also their proactive capacity expansion plans which makes
them attractive to AI customers as most of the other high-end CCL players are not

expanding enough capacity in 2027/28.
Maintain Buy on EMC / TUC with TP of NT$11,700 / NT$3,460, implying 134%/150%
upside vs. last close.

### Page 5

Investment Thesis & PT methodology and risks

Investment Thesis - Elite Material  
EMC, a key supplier of high-end HDI material (70%+ market share) and SLP material 
(90%+ market share), has been focusing on the high-speed switch/server CCL market for 
3-5+ years. We are positive on its market share trajectory in the server industry and 
expect EMC to increase its share from <10% in the Purley generation to 15-20%/20+% 
in the Whitley/Eagle Stream platforms. EMC is also proactively expanding its share in the 
switch market (40%/30%+ in 2024/23 vs. <5% before 2019), with an expanding AI 
customer base (only one 400G customer in 3Q20, to being one of the largest suppliers in 
2023), which should continue to benefit the company’s top/bottom line growth over 
time. We believe EMC’s leading position in the AI server CCL market should drive strong 
revenue growth and GM/OPM expansion in the long term, given that it is the major 
supplier for all Nvidia/Google/AWS AI server projects, and we expect it to maintain its 
leadership position. Considering its leading position in the high-end CCL industry, solid 
production skills and earnings growth outlook, we are Buy rated on the stock. With the 
shares trading at a 2026E P/E below AI server component suppliers and supported by a 
solid growth outlook, we view valuation as attractive. 
Key downside risks include: (1) RCC to replace all high-end smartphone HDI design; (2) 
rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker smartphone and server shipments; and 
(3) rising competition from mainland China peers.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Elite Material  
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$11,700 is based on 27x 2H27-1H28E P/E 
(in line with the peak P/E multiple for AI component suppliers in the past 3 years).  
Key downside risks:  (1) RCC to replace all high-end smartphone HDI design; (2) rising 
trade tensions, which could lead to weaker smartphone and server shipments; and (3) 
rising competition from mainland China peers. 
Investment Thesis - Taiwan Union Technology Corp. 
TUC is a key high-end global CCL supplier that focuses on M7+ grade high-speed CCL 
(key applications include high-end switches (100G+) and AI server materials), with 20%+ 
market share in the past two years. The company continues to expect its unit market 
share in the server industry to increase from 15% in Whitley and Purley processors to 
higher in the Eagle Stream generation, and plans to launch two types of products for 
low-end and high-end customers (T2A and T2C) to gain market share. Also, strong 
growth in 400G/800G switch shipments and new 800G switch shipments, scheduled for 
launch in 2025-27, should continue to be a key demand driver for TUC, in our view. 
Additionally, the company is working on high-end AI projects with CSP and enterprise 
players, which we believe should drive revenue momentum in the long term. We view the 
stock as undervalued vs. Taiwan CCL peers on a P/E basis and rate it Buy.  
Key downside risks include: (1) slower-than-expected share gains in the low-loss CCL 
segment; (2) rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker server and switch 
shipments globally; and (3) rising competition from mainland China peers.

Industry View In-Line
(-) PC OEMs and ODMs: Intel (covered by Joseph Moore) expects 2H26 PC units to
fall 20%+ y/y as OEMs aggressively cut inventories amid weaker sell-through; the
correction has accelerated over the past two months. This aligns with Morgan

Stanley's estimate at a ~25% y/y decline  (report link).
(+) Server OEMs and ODMs: Intel guides for server CPU unit growth of 30%+ in
both 2026 and 2027, with upside if wafer/substrate supply improves. It sees server
CPU TAM rising from US$40bn today to >US$200bn in five years, driven mainly by
AI agents.
(+) ABF substrates:  Most of the previously discussed ~40% capacity increase is due
in 2H27; Intel expects key component purchase commitments to rise sharply over

the next several quarters. Intel is also prioritizing securing substrate capacity and
improving yields for the EMIB-T. Complex designs add yield risk, which could lower
effective output, worsening ABF substrate shortages but adding further support for
pricing.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 179: 261104_ms_cotech

### Page 1

Idea
3Q preview: Co-Tech is scheduled to report its 3Q results on November 6. We
estimate 3Q revenue at NT$3,169mn (+13% q/q, +60% y/y), primarily driven by
higher copper prices and product-mix shifts. We lower our 3Q GM estimate from
28.2% to 27.0% (-30bps q/q, +650bps y/y), reflecting the impact of higher
production costs and copper prices during the quarter. Co-Tech has already reported
preliminary monthly profits for August and September at the request of the TWSE

(report link); combined August and September net profit now accounts for
approximately 67% of our 3Q estimate. We estimate 3Q net profit at NT$610mn
(+12% q/q, +141% y/y), implying EPS of NT$2.41.
4Q GM should improve sequentially: We expect 4Q revenue to decline slightly q/q
to NT$3,074mn (-3% q/q, +42% y/y), primarily due to the end of production for low-
end HTE copper foil and the lack of new capacity coming online. However, we expect
Co-Tech’s GM to expand 700bps q/q to 34%, driven by an improved product mix, a

~10% processing-fee increase effective from 4Q onward, and lower electricity rates
compared with 2Q–3Q, when summer electricity rates were in effect. We forecast
4Q EPS of NT$3.02 (+25% q/q, +102% y/y). For CY26, we estimate revenue will
increase approximately 47% y/y to NT$11.6bn, with GM expanding 760bps y/y to
29.3%, implying EPS of NT$9.62 (+129% y/y).
Potential processing-fee hike: Our supply-chain checks suggest that a key
competitor may announce processing-fee increases as early as December. If so, we

expect Co-Tech to follow with a price increase as well.
Upward revisions to our capacity assumptions ( Exhibit 3 ): Co-Tech has
announced plans to expand its greenfield capacity in Malaysia (report link). The
Malaysian expansion will be implemented in two phases, adding approximately 20k
tons of annual HVLP3/4 capacity from late 2028 onward. RG-series capacity is also
expected to increase modestly through the conversion of older HTE production
lines.

Raised PT to NT$745; stay OW: The increase in our price target is driven by our
earnings estimate changes for CY26-28. Our new price target implies 21x CY28 P/E
and the stock currently trades at only 12x CY28 P/E. We also find the valuation
attractive compared to  its past 3Y P/E average of ~20x.

Price target and valuation methodology
Our price target for Co-Tech increases  ~2% to NT$745: This implies 39x CY27 or 21x
CY28 P/E. Our price target is also our base case scenario value. It is derived from our

residual income (RI) model (similar to the rest of our tech hardware universe).
To calculate the fair value of the stock, we use the company's beginning equity plus the
present value of all expected residual income of earnings in excess of cost of capital. The
RI is positive when ROAE is above the cost of capital and negative when it is below the
cost of capital. Our key assumptions include:
•  A cost of equity of 9.3% (beta of 1.0, equity risk premium of 8.7%, and risk-free rate

of 1.0%),
• a medium-term growth rate of 11.5% (lowered from 13% previously, as we enter a
higher base in CY27),
• a terminal growth rate of 3%.
The 21x CY28 P/E implied by our price target is above its  past 3Y average  of 20x. We deem
this valuation as fair, because Co-Tech is benefitting from an earnings growth cycle fueled
by stronger AI server/networking CCL demand, Co-Tech's continued mix improvement
(from HTE + RTF (RG) copper foils to advanced RTF (RG) + HVLP3/4 copper foils) and

continued  processing fee adjustments, which supports margin expansion. The stock is
trading at just 12x CY28 P/E, below Co-Tech's past 3Y average of 20x. Thus, we view the
risk reward as attractive.
Our bull- and bear-case values also increase ~2% to NT$1,225 and NT$305,
respectively.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

3Q26 results overview 
AWSC’s 3Q26 core business OP was NT$209mn, which was lower 
than both GSe/BBG consensus of NT$226mn/251mn, mainly due to 
the ongoing impact from rising material cost, leading to a 24.3% GM 
(lower than GSe/BBG consensus GM of 25.6%/29.3%), despite UTR 
in 3Q26 remaining stable at 65-70%. 
For earnings, AWSC 3Q26 EPS was 3% higher than GSe but was 9% 
lower than BBG consensus on the contracted GM. 
Valuation 
We maintain our Neutral rating with a 12M TP of NT$147, based on 
27x 4Q26-3Q27E P/E, which is line with the company’s past 10-year 
forward P/E multiple, and in line with the inventory build-up period 
average P/E.

### Page 3

Investment Thesis and TP & risks

AWSC is one of the largest GaAS foundries globally, with 10-15% market share in the 
GaAS foundry industry in 2025. We expect the company to enjoy solid share gain 
opportunity in the long term, considering its much lower than peers’ production cost 
and better production efficiency. However, given the high inventory level in the supply 
chain, and the slower-than-expected Android smartphone PA demand in coming 
quarters, we expect relatively slow profit growth in the near term. 
We are Neutral rated given the company’s relatively slow profit growth in coming 
quarters. We think the stock is priced fairly, trading at its historical upcycle average P/E 
multiple, despite the long-term market share growth opportunity. We forecast AWSC’s 
net income to grow at a 34% 2026-28E CAGR. The key up/downside risks are (1) 
easing/rising competition in the China PA foundry market, (2) 
faster/slower-than-expected progress on 5G/WiFi 7 upgrade trends, and (3) 
faster/slower-than expected progress with new businesses, including LEO satellite, 
drones and datacom.  
Valuation methodology: Our 12-month target price of NT$147 is based on a target 27x 
4Q26-3Q27 P/E, which is in line with the company’s past 10-year forward P/E multiple 
and the inventory build-up period average P/E. We consider this multiple appropriate 
given the soft demand outlook and our positive view on the company’s longer-term 
share gain opportunity. 
The key up/downside risks are 1) easing/rising competition in the China PA foundry 
market, (2) faster/slower-than-expected progress on 5G/WiFi 7 upgrade trends, and (3) 
faster/slower-than expected progress with new businesses, including LEO satellite, 
drones and datacom.

물량 확대는 증익으로 귀결

Buy (유지)

주가 추이

견조한 판매 확대로 매출 견조, 이익은 시장 기대치 하회 예상 
3Q26 동사 매출액과 영업이익은 각각 33.6조원(+17.3% 이하 Y oY)과 2.39조
원(+63.6%) 기록 전망. 3Q 매출액은 친환경차 중심 신차 확대 통해 두 자릿수 
성장세를 기록. 3Q 판매량은 도매 기준 84.3만대(+7.6% 이하 Y oY)로 전년 동
기 대비 성장 폭을 확대했고, 특히 친환경차 판매량 29.8만대(+45.9%)를 기록
하며 이번 분기 매출 성장에 절대적으로 기여.  
다만, 3Q 수익성은 4~5월 상승했던 원자재 투입 영향과 ‘경제형EV 믹스’가 더
욱 확대(13.2% → 14.5%)되면서 단기 수익성에 부정적 작용하며 시장 기대치 
2.5조원을 다소 하회할 전망. 그러나, 기말 환율이 1,356원/$ 수준으로 낮 아 지
면서 전년 동기 수 익 성 악화에 크게 영 향 을 미쳤던 판매보증비 외화환산손익 
영향이 개선(1.6%p 수준) 기대되며, 관세 부담 또한 북미 HEV 확대로 전년 동
기 대비 약 4,500억원(2.0%p) 감소하면서 부정적 수익성 영향 만회 전망.  
유럽은 점유율 확대, 북미는 수익성 개선으로 
올해 북미 HEV와 유럽 BEV 중심 신차 출시 및 물량 확대가 본격화되면
서, ’27년에는 이익 성장 기여도가 확대될 것으로 전망. 미국 조 지 아 텔 루 라 이
드 HEV 신규 생산(2.8만대) 및 HMGMA 스 포 티 지HEV신규 생산(1.0만
대) 등 수 요 가 높은 북미 현 지 에 서 HEV 물량 확대 중으로 4Q에도 텔루라이
드 HEV와 스포티지 HEV의 현지 생산/판매 확대를 비롯하여 셀토스 HEV 
출시로 ’27년 북미 중심 HEV 신차 물량 증가 및 믹스 개선 기대. 유럽은 강한 
BEV 수요가 지속되면서 중국 업체들의 공세 강화 중이나 동사의 물량 확대 또
한 견조. 3Q EV3 2.9만대, EV5 2.2만대 등 유럽 BEV 수요 대응 전략 주효. ’27
년에는 K4/셀토스 및 쏘렌토 등 HEV 신차 중 심 의 친환경차 PT 믹스 다양화
로 유럽 내 물량 확대 흐름 지속 전망 
물량 확대는 증익으로 귀결, 기말 배당 매력도 부각 
’27년 동사 영업이익 추 정 치 는 11.1조원(+13.5%)으로 증익 지속 전망. 최근 
환율 추세 고려 시 내년 수익성에 부 정 적 영향 확대 가능성 존재하나, 올해 확
인된 신차 중심 물량 확대를 통해 부정적 환율 영향을 상쇄할 것 으 로 기대. 
12M EPS 추정치 하향(-2.9%) 및 상 반 기 적 용 한 코스피 프리미엄 제거(-
10%)로 목 표 주 가 를 260,000원으로 하향. 그러나, RA 설립에서의 동사 지분 
참여 및 기말 배당(’26년 DPS 7,000원 추정)에 따른 상 대 적 배당 수익 매력
도 부각 등은 주가 모 멘 텀 에 있어 기대 가능한 요소.

[ Compliance Notice ] (공표일: 2026 년 10월 8 일) 
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 8

기아 (000270) [한화리서치]

8 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 9 월 30 일) 
투자등급 매수 중립 매도 합계

### Page 1

기아 (000270)

EARNINGS PREVIEW Company Analysis | 자동차/로봇 | 2026.10.15  
잘 싸웠지만 아쉬운 3분기

3Q26 Preview 
기아는 3Q26E 매출액 31.1조원(+8.2% YoY, -6.0% QoQ), 영업이익 2.2조원
(+53.1% YoY, -14.9% QoQ, OPM 7.2%)을 기록, 컨센서스를 하회할 전망이다. 
물량은 좋았으나, 아쉬운 믹스와 환율 
글로벌 도매 판매 84.7만대(+7.8% 이하 YoY), 중국 제외 판매량은 83.0만대
(+8.6%)를 기록했다. EV·HEV 확대에 따른 ASP 상승은 지속되고 있다. 전쟁으로 인
한 원자재 상승으로 3분기 재료비 부담은 전분기 대비 3,000억~4,000억원 증가할 
것으로 예상된다. EV 판매 비중은 2분기 13%에서 3분기 15%, 유럽은 35%에서 
43%로 확대되며 유럽 내 마진 희석은 불가피하다. 원화 강세에 따른 영업상 부담은 
판매보증충당부채 환입 등으로 상쇄돼 3분기 환율 영향은 제한적일 전망이다. 판매보
증충당부채 환입은 QoQ +5,000억원 수준으로 추정된다. 4분기부터 환율 효과는 부정
적일 것이며, 환율 10원 변동 시 연간 손익 민감도는 약 1,500억원 수준이다. 
투자의견은 Buy를 유지하고 목표주가는 165,000원으로 하향 
미국 조지아 공장의 텔루라이드 HEV(2.8만대)와 HMGMA의 스포티지 HEV(1.0만대) 
신규 생산으로 북미의 견조한 HEV 수요에 대응하며, 2027년 믹스 개선이 기대된다. 
유럽은 EV3·EV5로 점유율을 확대하는 가운데 유럽 내 할인율 하락과 EV 잔존가치 
회복으로 판매 여건이 개선되고 있다. 향후 IAA 규제에 따른 법인·플릿 혜택 제한은 
중국 업체의 원가 우위를 줄일 수 있을 전망이다. 
원화 약세 지속을 반영해 이익 추정치를 하향하며, 이에 따라 목표주가를 하향한다.  
다만, 현재 주가는 12M Fwd P/E 기준 4.8배로 Legacy OEM 평균(7.7배)보다 낮은 
수준, 저가 매수 기회라 판단된다. 현재 배당 수익률은 6% 수준으로, 상대적으로 높은 
배당 매력은 주가 하방을 지지할 전망이다. 
 Analyst 이병근 
bglee@ls-sec.co.kr

## Document 297: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 업종 내 여전히 매력적인 선택지HD현대중공업(329180) 업종 내 여전히 매력적인 선택지업종 내 여전히 매력적인 선택지 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 업종 내 여전히 매력적인 선택지HD현대중공업(329180) 업종 내 여전히 매력적인 선택지업종 내 여전히 매력적인 선택지 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-15
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

HD현대중공업의 3Q26 연결 실적은 매출액 5조 8,602억원(yoy +33%, qoq -7%), 
영업이익 9,161억원(yoy +64%, qoq -12%, OPM 15.6%)으로 시장 컨센서스를 하회할 
전망이다. 동사의 3Q26 조업일수는 여름휴가, 추석연휴 및 중대재해 등 영향으로 
qoq -21% 감소했으며 환율 역시 qoq -84원 감소한 영향을 감안해 전분기 대비 
실적을 보수적으로 추정했다. 그럼에도 해양 부문 Trion FPU발 C/O에 따른 일회성 
환입이 예상됨에 따라 일부 이익 상쇄가 가능할 것으로 예상된다. 한편, 동사의 
임단협 부결로 당분기 추가적인 인건비 부담은 제한적일 것으로 판단되며 잠수함 
화재 사고 관련 비용 역시 4Q 이후 반영될 전망이다.

동사는 급격히 커져가는 AIDC향 전력수요 및 발전용 엔진 수주에 대응하고자 9/10 
총 4GW 규모의 4행정 중속엔진 캐파 증설을 위한 신규 투자를 공시헸다. 동사의 
기존 DC향 4행정 중속엔진 수주 단가를 고려 시 GW당 약 1조원의 매출 창출이 
가능할 전망이며 2행정 및 선박용 4행정 엔진 대비 높은 수익성이 예상되는 바 
생산이 본격화될 28년부터 동사 실적 성장성의 핵심 동인으로 작용할 전망이다.

동사에 대해 투자의견 BUY를 유지하며 업종 밸류에이션 디레이팅 및 시장 프리미엄 
조정에 따른 Target Multipe 변동으로 목표주가를 70만원으로 하향 조정한다. 상선 
시장 수주 경쟁 심화로 업종 밸류 조정이 이루어지고 있으나 동사는 엔진 기반 장기 
성장성으로 Peer 대비 상대적 매력도 확보 및 주가 방어가 가능하다는 판단이다.

## Document 319: AI監管衝擊與選後美股展望-CTBC20261106

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI監管衝擊與選後美股展望-CTBC20261106.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-06
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

AI
監管衝擊與選後美股展望
2026.11.06
美股策略研究
:

厲柏宏

### Page 2

結論與展望
• AI監管風險：民主黨取得國會兩院控制權，AI監管疑慮升溫。2027年資料中心建設進度相對
穩定，但2028年約30GW規劃容量面臨潛在審批風險，恐影響部分專案投產時程。
• 產業影響預期：德州共和黨仍掌握州參議院多數，長期全面禁建面臨立法及司法制衡。過去
約20GW受地方建設限制的容量中，實際延期僅約1.5GW，顯示政策曝險不等於實際損失。
• 政策影響：民主黨可能透過國會制衡財政擴張，中長期若赤字及發債壓力下降，將有利美債
表現。美元預期短空長多，而美伊衝突降溫預期升高，可能使短期油價持續承壓。
• 醫療與零售：民主黨可能爭取恢復部分Medicaid、SNAP及ACA補貼，有助維持中低收入家
庭購買力，支撐消費與醫療需求；惟川普仍掌握關稅行政權，零售業成本改善空間相對有限。
• 美股後市展望：歷史經驗顯示，期中選舉後隨政治不確定性下降，美股通常呈現回升。過去9
次期中選舉後12個月，科技股有8次跑贏大盤，獲利品質、成長及低波動策略亦具相對優勢，
後續仍須關注AI監管落地及企業獲利表現。

1資料來源：中信投顧整理

### Page 3

AI監管提案增加，2028面臨較高審批風險
• 民主黨在期中選舉取得參眾兩院 ，並提出超級 AI禁令及資料中心暫停建設法案，分別涉及限
制AI模型開發，及暫停新建資料中心直至相關機制建立 ，引發對監管趨嚴與投資放緩的擔憂 。
• SemiAnalysis估計，美國2027預計交付的資料中心約有75%已動工，短期進度穩定；惟2028，
規劃多數尚未施工，若暫停建設政策擴大，約30GW新增容量可能面臨審批延後風險。
2資料來源：dcmap.us、中信投顧整理
AI監管風險
美國各州資料中心容量分布與建設進度 (GW)

### Page 4

州級監管受立法制衡，政策曝險不等於實際損失
• 州長行政權仍受州議會及司法制衡 ，以德州為例，共和黨仍掌握州參議院過半席次，全面禁
建制度化仍需跨黨派支持；惟州政府可延續部分許可限制，短期專案進度仍可能受影響。
• 過去一年約20GW受地方建設限制的容量中，實際延誤僅約 1.5GW；預期監管趨嚴將提高開
發成本並延後部分2028專案，短期影響AI硬體訂單與交付，但長期算力需求未必減少。
3資料來源：MultiState Elctions、SemiAnalysis、中信投顧整理
產業影響預期
11/05德州期中選舉統計 地方禁令實際影響有限

### Page 5

國會制衡與美伊局勢緩和，牽動美債及美元走勢
• 民主黨掌控國會後，較可能透過立法及預算權制衡共和黨政策 ，降低進一步財政擴張空間。
短期政策不確定性可能加大債市波動 ，但中長期若赤字與發債壓力下降，將有利美債表現。
而AI投資貢獻美國經濟成長比例加大，故美元表現將與AI股趨勢相同，預期美元短空長多。
• 伊朗戰爭推升汽油價格成為共和黨選舉壓力來源 ，隨民主黨掌控國會提高延續軍事行動的政
治成本，加上美伊持續透過中東國家間接接觸，市場對衝突降溫預期升高，油價短期回落。
4資料來源：中信投顧整理
政策影響
美國總統與國會權力

### Page 6

國會控制權轉向，醫療與消費政策迎來調整空間
• 民主黨可能透過債務上限協商，爭取延後或縮減 Medicaid、SNAP減支，並恢復ACA增額保
費補貼，有助維持中低收入家庭購買力與醫療保險覆蓋率，對民生消費、醫療服務偏正面。
• 關稅也是本次輪替重點，但川普仍握有貿易政策行政權與301條款等工具，整體有效關稅稅率
未必明顯下降；因此零售業獲利改善仍取決於企業定價能力及供應鏈調整成效。
5資料來源：Commonwealth Fund、Uni. of Penn、中信投顧整理
醫療與零售
現行法規預期刪減福利 美國現行關稅預估

### Page 7

期中選舉後政治不確定性下降，歷史上美股通常回升
• 觀察過去數次美國期中選舉，美股通常在選前因政治不確定性升高而面臨較大波動 ，但選後
隨著政策方向逐漸明朗，期中選舉後標普多數呈現上漲趨勢。
• 產業方面，科技股在過去9次期中選舉後的12個月，科技股有8次跑贏標普。平均報酬最高的
選股策略則為獲利品質、成長與低波動類股。
6資料來源：JPM、Barclays、中信投顧整理

### Page 2

Weekly Economics
U.S. Week Ahead
Existing Home Sales • Thursday
Housing market conditions remain highly unfavorable. Mortgage
rates are the primary headwind, and they have climbed even higher
alongside the rise in longer-term yields. At the same time, low
supply is preventing a broad correction in home prices, leaving
prospective buyers squeezed on two fronts.
We expect that existing home sales slid 3.4% in August to an
annualized rate of 3.92 million. Behind this forecast is ongoing

weakness in mortgage applications and a recent bounce in
mortgage rates, which rose from 6.43% at the beginning of July to
6.66% by month-end.
We do not expect affordability conditions to meaningfully improve
in the near term. Supply is likely to remain constrained while high
mortgage rates discourage mobility. And mortgage rates are apt
to remain elevated amid firm economic growth expectations and
heavy government and corporate debt issuance.

### Page 3

Economics Week Ahead Economics
CPI • Friday
Headline inflation looks to have picked up last month. We estimate
the CPI rose 0.40% in August, with gasoline prices increasing a little
over 4% as renewed tensions in the Middle East pushed oil higher.
Grocery prices also likely rebounded after modestly declining in July.
Outside of food and energy, price pressures appear little changed.
We expect the core CPI to rise 0.23%, essentially matching July's

pace. Core goods inflation should remain firm, led by another solid
increase in used vehicle prices as consumer prices continue to
catch up to the trend indicated at wholesale auctions. Meantime,
household furnishings and other tariff-sensitive categories appear
to be stabilizing after declining earlier this year.
Core services inflation should be little changed with another 0.2%
monthly increase. But underneath the surface, we expect a modest
pickup in travel (led by lodging away from home) to offset slightly

cooler medical care and housing cost growth.
All told, we expect the report to show that while headline inflation
remains influenced by the ebbs and flows of the conflict in the
Middle East, core inflation remains contained. Should our forecast
prove correct, core CPI will ease to 2.4% year-over-year, in line with
its 6-month annualized rate but above the more recent 3-month
annualized pace of 1.7%.

### Page 4

Weekly Economics
G10 Week Ahead
European Central Bank Monetary Policy Meeting •
Thursday
We expect the European Central Bank (ECB) to raise the Deposit
Rate by 25 bps to 2.50% next week. August headline inflation
remained elevated at 3.3% year-over-year, though softer core
and services inflation suggest underlying pressures continue to
moderate. Still, the more hawkish tone of the July minutes and
renewed rise in energy prices should keep policymakers cautious.

The recent escalation in the Middle East has pushed fuel prices
at the pump close to their 2022 peaks, while natural gas storage
remains below historical levels, leaving the Eurozone vulnerable
to further energy price increases. The ECB is unlikely to respond
aggressively if the shock remains confined to energy given the
downside risks to growth. However, a broader rise in food inflation
linked to El Niño would be more concerning, as highly visible prices

could lift inflation expectations and generate second-round effects.
For now, resilient Q3 activity, elevated headline inflation and
contained wage growth support one final hike. The ECB will likely
retain and emphasize its scenario-based outlook in the updated
staff projections given continued uncertainty around the conflict
and energy prices. The policy path beyond September will depend
on whether energy and food pressures persist and begin to spread,

which could leave room for further tightening.
(Return)

U.K. Monthly GDP • Friday
We expect UK GDP to rise 0.2% month-over-month in July, with
growth of 0.3% on a three-month-over-three-month basis.

Activity likely moderated after June’s strong rebound, though
services remained supportive as the July services PMI rose to 52.1.
Retail sales volumes fell 0.5% month-over-month, while softer
manufacturing activity likely reflected some reversal of earlier
precautionary stockbuilding.
The moderation appears more consistent with payback from
June than a broader deterioration in momentum. August PMIs
remain consistent with Q3 GDP growth of around 0.3% quarter-

over-quarter. Still, the renewed escalation in the Middle East and
associated rise in gas and energy prices adds upside risk to inflation
and could weigh on household demand. Against this backdrop and
with Bank of England hawks continuing to emphasize the need for
tighter policy, we expect the BoE to remain cautious and continue
to look for a rate hike in Q4. -1.0%

### Page 5

Economics Week Ahead Economics
EM Week Ahead
Mexico CPI • Wednesday
We expect Mexico’s August inflation rate to edge higher, with
headline CPI rising to 3.34% year-over-year (0.27% month-over-
month) from 3.26% year-over-year in early August. Part of the
pickup likely reflects less favorable base effects and a smaller
disinflationary contribution from food prices. While agricultural
prices continued to help restrain inflation in mid-August, the drag

was notably less pronounced than in July. Core inflation should
remain elevated at 3.98% year-over-year (0.25% month-over-
month).
While inflation remains within Banxico’s midpoint 3% target,
persistent core and services inflation suggest convergence toward
the midpoint of the target could prove gradual. For now, we
continue to expect Banxico to keep rates on hold, with the policy
rate remaining at 6.50% through the rest of 2026 and into early
2027 as policymakers await clearer evidence that underlying

inflation pressures are easing sustainably. While rate cuts remain
a possibility in 2027, the current inflation backdrop argues for
patience in the near term.

Brazil CPI • Friday
We expect Brazil’s August inflation rate to slow to 4.20% year-over-
year, helped by lower electricity, food and fuel prices. The main
driver should be a temporary reduction in residential electricity bills
stemming from a one-off credit, while recent data already point to
a notable easing in price pressures. Mid-August CPI came in weaker
than expected, with inflation falling to 4.24% year-over-year amid
declines in electricity, food, fuel and airfare prices. Beyond these

temporary factors, restrictive monetary policy continues to weigh
on economic activity, as evidenced by the recent slowdown in Q2
GDP growth. However, underlying inflation risks remain. A still-
tight labor market and government measures aimed at supporting
consumption could keep services inflation sticky, making progress
toward the 3.0% inflation target more gradual.
Overall, the inflation outlook remains consistent with additional
policy easing. We expect COPOM to deliver a 25 bps rate cut in

September, bringing the Selic Rate to 13.75%. Beyond September,
policymakers are likely to proceed cautiously as they assess
incoming inflation data and the impact of the election cycle on
inflation expectations. Our base case remains for a pause after
September, with additional easing more likely in 2027.

## Document 380: FOMC 明確さを待つために様子見

Source PDF: all report pdf/FOMC　明確さを待つために様子見.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期

### Page 1

米国

株式会社大和総研 丸の内オフィス 〒100-6756 東京都千代田区丸の内一丁目 9 番 1 号 グラントウキョウ ノースタワー

2026 年 10 月 29 日 全5 頁

FOMC 明確さを待つために様子見 
FOMC はインフレ高止まり、景気下振れ、金融面のリスクに直面

経済調査部 主任研究員 矢作 大祐 
ニューヨークリサーチセンター 研究員 藤原 翼

[要約] 
 2026 年10 月のFOMC では、FF 金利の誘導目標レンジを3.75～4.00％に据え置いた。市
場は事前にFF 金利の据え置きを織り込んでおり、決定後の長期金利、株価、ドルにも
大きな変動は見られなかった。 
 声明文では景気判断を上方修正し、ウォーシュFRB 議長も FOMC 後の記者会見で、個人
消費、設備投資、雇用環境の底堅さを強調した。そして、インフレ率は依然高すぎる
ことも引き続き強調し た。他方、金融環境に関するウォーシュ議長の指摘には変化が
見られた。資本配分の選別が進み、一部資産では信用スプレッドが急拡大するなど、
金融環境には引き締まりの兆候が表れているとした。 
 もっとも、ウォーシュ議長は金融環境の引き締まりの 程度を断定するのは時期尚早と
の見解を示した。ウォーシュ議長は今後のデータによって状況がより「明確」になる
と述べ、フォワードガイダンスの提示には慎重な姿勢を維持しつつも、12月のFOMC で
利上げに踏み切る可能性をにじませたと解釈すべきである。 
 9 月時点のFOMC 参加者の金利見通しと直近のFedWatch は、12 月のFOMC での0.25％pt
追加利上げを示唆している。しかし、実施の可否は予断を許さない。注目点は、期待
インフレ率の上振れ、雇用統計、とりわけ労働参加率の改善、社債利回りなどに表れ
る金融ストレスの 3 点である。FOMC は、利上げによる景気・金融環境の悪化と、据え
置きによるインフレ期待の上振れというジレンマに直面しており、政策判断とともに
丁寧な情報発信が求められる。

### Page 2

1

2 / 5

政策金利の据え置きを決定 
2026 年 10 月 27 日・28 日に開催された FOMC（連邦公開市場委員会）では、政策金利である
FF（フェデラルファンド）レートの誘導目標レンジを 3.75～4.00％に据え置いた。なお、今回
の決定について、クックFRB 理事とクリーブランド連銀のハマック総裁が0.25％pt の利上げを
主張して反対票を投じた。 
今回の決定は、市場にとってサプライズとはならなかった。CME が算出する FedWatch（FF 先
物市場から算出される利上げ・利下げ確率）によると、FOMC の開催直前（10 月 26 日）の時点
で、金利据え置きの確率が約 90％となっていた。前回（2026 年 9 月 15 日・16 日）の FOMC で
0.25％pt の利上げが実施された後、FOMC 参加者は根強いインフレを背景に年内の追加利上げを
主張する一方、利上げの時期に関しては慎重に評価していくとの見解を示していたことから、
市場も金利据え置きを織り込んでいた。 
なお、FOMC 後の記者会見において、ウォーシュ FRB 議長は来年以降における FOMC の開催ス
ケジュールに言及した。具体的には、今後も 1 年あたり合計 8 回の会合を行う一方で、そのう
ち 2027 年は 1 回分、2028 年は 2 回分を金融政策の広範な戦略に特化した会合に充てるとし、
金融政策の決定や声明文の公表は行わないと発表した。

声明文・記者会見：金融環境の変化を指摘する一方、慎重に評価を行う意向 
今回公表された声明文を見ると、冒頭で「委員会は10 対2 の賛成多数で以下の声明の公表を
承認した」と前置きした上 で、「委員会は、連邦準備制度の二重の目標（デュアルマンデート）
を支えるため、FF 金利の目標レンジを3.75～4.00％に維持することを決定した。委員会は、銀
行システムにおける潤沢な準備預金を維持する政策を継続する」と決定内容を示した。 
続いて、景気認識については、「経済活動は力強く拡大している。地政学の動向などにより
不確実性は依然として高いものの、国内支出は底堅く推移している。生産性の伸びは力強く、
設備投資も堅調だ」と、前回の「経済活動は堅調に拡大」から「経済活動は力強く拡大」に上
方修正された。雇用環境については、 「雇用者数の増加は労働力人口の増加に追いついており、
失業率はほとんど変化していない」との表現が据え置かれた。ウォーシュ議長は記者会見で、
2026 年上半期の実質GDP 成長率が上方修正され、第3 四半期も力強い成長が見込まれると指摘
した。さらに、個人消費は堅調で、設備投資は、けん引役となっているハイテク関連だけでは
なく、それ以外の項目にも波及が見られると述べた。他方でウォーシュ議長は、多くのFOMC 参
加者は景気が過熱しているとは評価しておらず、その要因として 、自身の見立てでは生産性が
改善していることに言及した。また、雇用環境については、新規失業保険申請件数が低水準で
推移していると指摘し、極めて良好と評価した。 
物価関連の記述を見ると、 「インフレ率は依然として高水準にある。本日の政策措置は、委
員会が掲げる 2％目標への早期回帰を支援するものである。委員会は物価安定を実現する」と

### Page 3

1

3 / 5

の表現から「インフレ率は依然として高水準にある。より多くの情報が集積されることで、委
員会はインフレ率を目標の 2％に速やかに戻すための適切な政策スタンスを決定できるだろう。
委員会は物価安定を実現する」に変更された。ウォーシュ議長は記者会見で、インフレ率は依
然として高すぎると指摘した。さらに、直近数週間においてエネルギー価格やそれに関連する
価格が再上昇していることに警戒感を示した。もっとも、ウォーシュ議長は FRB の取り組みを
評価する指標として期待インフレ率を挙げ、現時点では正しい方向に進んでいるとの認識を示
した。 
また、今回の記者会見では、金融環境についての質問が目立った。ウォーシュ議長は、一部
の企業や家計において金融環境は引き締まりつつあるとの見解を示し、 市場参加者は資本配分
について以前よりも慎重かつ選別的な姿勢をやや強めていると言及した。足元で注目される AI
関連投資の資金調達を含め、金利は幅広い分野に直接・間接の影響を及ぼしているとの認識を
示した。9 月初め以降、市場参加者が投資対象をより選別するようになり、一部の資産では信
用スプレッドが急拡大し、その他の資産でも、金融環境は大幅ではないものの、幾分引き締ま
っていると指摘した。 
もっとも、ウォーシュ議長は、金融環境は引き締まりつつあるものの、現時点で「引き締ま
った状態にある」と判断するのは時期尚早との見方を示した。その上で、今後得られるデータ
によって状況がより「明確（more clarity ）」になると述べた。 この「明確」という表現は、
様々な情報を把握するという目的で様子見を決定した今回のFOMC とは対照的な表現と捉えるべ
きだろう。とりわけ、ウォーシュ議長はフォワードガイダンスを示すことに慎重な姿勢を続け
ていることを踏まえれば、自身のそうした姿勢と矛盾しない範囲で、12 月の FOMC で利上げに
踏み切る姿勢をにじませたといえるのではないか。

FOMC はインフレ高止まり、景気下振れ、金融面のリスクに直面 
今回のFOMC では利上げをせず、様子見となった。市場でも据え置きが想定されていたことか
ら、予想通りの結果といえる。10 年債金利や主要株価指数、ドル指数などにも大きな変動は見
られなかった。 
直近のCPI、PPI が市場予想を上回るなどインフレ懸念が強まる中でも、様子見となった背景
には、景気の下振れリスクへの警戒感があるだろう。ウォーシュ議長は景気を堅調と表現した
が、経済指標を見ると、9 月の雇用統計の雇用者数や小売売上高に減速感が見られるなど、屋
台骨である個人消費には下振れリスクがある。また、けん引役の設備投資も CoreWeave 問題な
どを背景に、債務の持続可能性が警戒されており、 社債利回りは上昇している 1。金融環境全
体に関して、OFR（米財務省金融リサーチ室） の金融ストレス指数に注目すると、足元で上昇
（ストレスの強まり）傾向が見られる（図表 1 左図）。内訳を見ると、ボラティリティ要因がプ

1 矢作大祐・藤原翼「米国経済見通し CoreWeave 問題をどうみるか」（大和総研レポート、2026 年10 月 22
日）

### Page 4

4 / 5

ラスに転じ、クレジット要因のマイナス幅も縮小している。金融環境にストレスが見られつつ
ある中で、FOMC 参加者は様子見姿勢を強めたと考えられる。 
では、先行きの金融政策はどう考えればよいか。9月のFOMC で公表された、FOMC 参加者のFF
金利見通し（中央値）では、2026 年12 月末時点でFF 金利は4.1％となっている。10 月のFOMC
でFF 金利が据え置かれたことから、FOMC 参加者の予想通りであれば、12 月のFOMC での0.25％
pt 追加利上げが想定される。なお、10 月 29 日時点の FedWatch でも 12 月の FOMC では 0.25％
pt の利上げ確率が高いと予想されている。また、ウォーシュ議長が FOMC 後の記者会見で今後
得られるデータによって状況がより明確になると述べたことについて、利上げの可能性をにじ
ませたと解釈すべきとしたのは前述の通りだ。 
図表 1 金融ストレス指数、期待インフレ率の期間構造

（注）左図の金融ストレス指数は上昇すると、金融ストレスの強まりを示す。 
（出所）OFR、クリーブランド連銀、Haver Analytics より大和総研作成

他方で、実際に 12 月の FOMC で利上げを実施できるか否かは予断を許さない。利上げの可能
性を考える上では、3 つの注目点を取り上げたい。1 つ目の注目点として、インフレ率の高止ま
りリスクに関連して、期待インフレ率に注目したい。ウォーシュ議長はFOMC 後の記者会見で、
現時点で期待インフレ率が正しい方向に向かっていると評価したが、クリーブランド連銀が算
出した期待インフレ率の期間構造を見ると、2026 年 10 月は 2026 年 9 月に比べて上方シフトし
た（図表1 右図）。期待インフレ率の上方シフトがさらに進めば、アンカリング機能が低下して
いるとの懸念から、FOMC が利上げ姿勢を強めると考えられる。なお、11 月分の期待インフレ率
に関しては、11 月10 日に公表が予定されている。 
他方で、2 つ目の注目点として、実体経済の下振れリスクに関連して、11 月6 日や12 月4 日
に公表予定の雇用統計、とりわけ労働参加率が挙げられる 2。労働参加率は 8 月以降上昇に転

じた。労働需給や賃金上昇圧力の緩和だけでなく、消費者の雇用マインドの下支えという意味
でも労働参加率の改善が継続するかが重要となろう。そして、3 つ目の注目点は、金融環境だ。
前述の通り、社債利回りといったクレジット要因の悪化に歯止めがかかるかが焦点となる。 
FOMC は、根強いインフレ圧力に対して利上げで対処すれば景気・金融環境を悪化させかねず、
反対に据え置けばインフレ期待の上振れを招きかねないというジレンマに直面しているといえ
る。こうした状況では、政策判断そのものに加え、市場の利上げ 期待の過度な変動を招かない
よう、FOMC 参加者の丁寧なコミュニケーションも重要となろう。

⚫ 3Q26營收增幅低於預期

3Q26營收增幅低於預期： M31 3Q26營收為 4.95億元 (QoQ+17.4%，
YoY+39.6%) ，以美金計價營收為 1,546 萬美元 (QoQ+16.2% ，
YoY+31.0%)，營收成長受惠於客戶開案動能強勁、權利金穩健成長，
以及北美高價值專案營收認列。營業費用為 4.8 億元 (QoQ+11.2%，
YoY+21.1%)，因調整部分獎金發放時點而使本季營業費用較高。營業
利益為 730萬元，相較 2Q26由虧轉盈。 EPS為 0.35元。  
3Q26授權金與權利金季增福皆達雙位數： 3Q26授權金收入為 4.06億
元(QoQ+17%，YoY+47%)，約佔營收 82%，創歷史單季第三高。 Fabless
授權金收入以美元計算 QoQ+3%，YoY+127%，主要由北美客戶先進
製程專案及晶圓代工平台合作案帶動，其中包含 公司首個 2奈米以下
的 GAA架構專案，Fabless 客戶持續提升對 16奈米以下先進製程技術
的採用比例，先進製程專案已成為授權業務成長的主要驅動力。 3Q26
權利金收入為 0.89 億元 (QoQ+19%，YoY+14%)，約佔營收 18%，創
1Q23以來新高，為近 15季最佳表現， Foundry 權利金收入持續成長，
反映既有平台專案逐步放量並持續貢獻營收，未來 A14、2奈米及 CSP
等新案機會可望進一步延續公司於先進製程領域的成長動能。  
提升權利金比重，使整體營收分布更為平均： 公司將於 4Q26 持續深
化與北美 IDM客戶客製化記憶體平台、美系晶圓廠 12奈米平台的合
作，完成與大型晶圓廠 28奈米 Foundation IP 合約簽署， 2奈米以下
與多項先進製程專案持續開展， 將帶動授權金維持高檔。公司目標
2026年 EPS轉正，且全年營收較 2025年成長。 2027年授權金目標成
長約 10%，權利金挑戰 30%以上成長，隨著更多客戶專案逐步進入大
量生產階段，預期先進製程權利金收入將進入加速成長期， 16奈米以
下先進製程權利金比重將提升至 50%。預期 2027 年整體營收分布更
為平穩，目標上下半年分別為 45:55。 
我們基於 2027年預估 EPS 6.35 元*60倍 PER，調整目標價至 381元，
維持增加持股評等。 風險因子 :專案進程與製程平台導入進度。
